  1053	    async def hybrid_search(
  1054	        self,
  1055	        query: str,
  1056	        user_level: int,
  1057	        limit: int = 5,
  1058	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] = None,
  1059	        collection_override: str | None = None,
  1060	        apply_filters: bool | None = None,
  1061	        fallback_to_plain: bool = True,
  1062	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1063	        """
  1064	        Hybrid search combining dense vectors and BM25 sparse vectors.
  1065	
  1066	        Uses Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) to combine results from both
  1067	        dense semantic search and BM25 keyword search for optimal retrieval.
  1068	
  1069	        Results are cached in Redis (TTL 1h) keyed by query+user_level+limit+collection.
  1070	        Cache reduces avg latency from ~894ms to <100ms for repeated queries.
  1071	
  1072	        Args:
  1073	            query: Search query
  1074	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
  1075	            limit: Max results
  1076	            tier_filter: Optional specific tier filter
  1077	            collection_override: Force specific collection
  1078	            apply_filters: If True, apply tier/exclude_repealed filters
  1079	            fallback_to_plain: If True (default, historical behavior), any
  1080	                error in the hybrid path falls back to `self.search()` —
  1081	                which runs LLM query expansion (Gemini, via QueryExpander).
  1082	                Pass False on paths with a zero-LLM contract (the WA
  1083	                codex-route package builder, BOT-V4 S2): the error is
  1084	                re-raised so the caller degrades WITHOUT an LLM ever being
  1085	                reachable from this call.
  1086	
  1087	        Returns:
  1088	            Search results with hybrid search metadata
  1089	        """
  1090	        # --- Cache lookup ---
  1091	        from backend.core.cache import get_cache_service
  1092	
  1093	        cache = get_cache_service()
  1094	        cache_key = cache._generate_key(
  1095	            "hybrid_search",
  1096	            query,
  1097	            user_level=user_level,
  1098	            limit=limit,
  1099	            collection_override=collection_override or "",
  1100	        )
  1101	        cached_result = await cache.get(cache_key)
  1102	        if cached_result is not None:
  1103	            cached_result["cache_hit"] = True
  1104	            # B1.1: a cached entry written before score_provenance existed
  1105	            # (or by a legacy code path) carries no score_kind — declare it
  1106	            # UNKNOWN rather than leaving the field absent. No number moves.
  1107	            for entry in cached_result.get("results", []):
  1108	                if (
  1109	                    isinstance(entry, dict)
  1110	                    and score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY not in entry
  1111	                ):
  1112	                    score_provenance.stamp(entry, score_provenance.UNKNOWN)
  1113	            return cached_result
  1114	
  1115	        try:
  1116	            # Prepare search context (same as regular search)
  1117	            embedding_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1118	            (
  1119	                query_embedding,
  1120	                collection_name,
  1121	                vector_db,
  1122	                chroma_filter,
  1123	                _tier_values,
  1124	            ) = await self._prepare_search_context(
  1125	                query,
  1126	                user_level,
  1127	                tier_filter,
  1128	                collection_override,
  1129	                apply_filters,
  1130	            )
  1131	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE and embedding_start:
  1132	                rag_embedding_duration.observe(time.time() - embedding_start)
  1133	
  1134	            # Generate BM25 sparse vector
  1135	            query_sparse = None
  1136	            if self._bm25_vectorizer:
  1137	                query_sparse = self._bm25_vectorizer.generate_query_sparse_vector(query)
  1138	                logger.debug(
  1139	                    f"Generated BM25 sparse vector: {len(query_sparse.get('indices', []))} tokens",
  1140	                )
  1141	
  1142	            # Try hybrid search if available
  1143	            search_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1144	            if query_sparse and hasattr(vector_db, "hybrid_search"):
  1145	                raw_results = await vector_db.hybrid_search(
  1146	                    query_embedding=query_embedding,
  1147	                    query_sparse=query_sparse,
  1148	                    filter=chroma_filter,
  1149	                    limit=limit,
  1150	                    prefetch_limit=limit * 3,  # Get more candidates for fusion
  1151	                )
  1152	                search_type = raw_results.get("search_type", "hybrid_rrf")
  1153	                # B1.1 / ruling R1: score_kind is DECLARED by this branch,
  1154	                # never derived from the (known-lossy) search_type default
  1155	                # above. `vector_db.hybrid_search()` stamps its own native
  1156	                # RRF-shaped dict with a "search_type" key on every path
  1157	                # (success and its own empty-result error path) — the ONE
  1158	                # case that key is absent is when it silently degraded to
  1159	                # `self.search()` internally (core/qdrant_db.py:1362),
  1160	                # whose dense-shaped dict never carries that key. Checking
  1161	                # the key's PRESENCE (not its value) tells hybrid from
  1162	                # dense here without touching `search_type` itself.
  1163	                declared_kind = (
  1164	                    score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED
  1165	                    if "search_type" in raw_results
  1166	                    else score_provenance.DENSE_FORMATTED
  1167	                )
  1168	            else:
  1169	                # Fallback to dense-only search
  1170	                use_vector_name = "dense" if _uses_named_vectors(collection_name) else None
  1171	                raw_results = await vector_db.search(
  1172	                    query_embedding=query_embedding,
  1173	                    filter=chroma_filter,
  1174	                    limit=limit,
  1175	                    vector_name=use_vector_name,
  1176	                )
  1177	                search_type = "dense_only"
  1178	                declared_kind = score_provenance.DENSE_FORMATTED
  1179	
  1180	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE and search_start:
  1181	                rag_vector_search_duration.observe(time.time() - search_start)
  1182	
  1183	            # Format results
  1184	            formatted_results = format_search_results(
  1185	                raw_results,
  1186	                collection_name,
  1187	                primary_collection=None,
  1188	                query=query,
  1189	                score_kind=declared_kind,
  1190	            )
  1191	
  1192	            # Record query for health monitoring
  1193	            avg_score = (
  1194	                sum(r["score"] for r in formatted_results) / len(formatted_results)
  1195	                if formatted_results
  1196	                else 0.0
  1197	            )
  1198	            self.health_monitor.record_query(
  1199	                collection_name=collection_name,
  1200	                had_results=len(formatted_results) > 0,
  1201	                result_count=len(formatted_results),
  1202	                avg_score=avg_score,
  1203	            )
  1204	
  1205	            result = {
  1206	                "query": query,
  1207	                "results": formatted_results,
  1208	                "collection": collection_name,
  1209	                "total_results": len(formatted_results),
  1210	                "search_type": search_type,
  1211	                "bm25_enabled": query_sparse is not None,
  1212	            }
  1213	
  1214	            # --- Cache write (non-blocking, TTL from redis_manager config) ---
  1215	            if formatted_results:
  1216	                try:
  1217	                    await cache.set(cache_key, result)
  1218	                except Exception as cache_err:
  1219	                    logger.debug("Cache write failed (non-critical): %s", cache_err)
  1220	
  1221	            return result
  1222	
  1223	        except Exception as e:
  1224	            logger.error("Hybrid search error: %s", e, exc_info=True)
  1225	            if not fallback_to_plain:
  1226	                # Zero-LLM contract path (BOT-V4 S2): `self.search()` runs
  1227	                # Gemini query expansion, so falling back here would put an
  1228	                # LLM call on a path that promised none. Re-raise instead —
  1229	                # the caller owns the degradation.
  1230	                raise
  1231	            # Fallback to regular search on error
  1232	            return await self.search(
  1233	                query=query,
  1234	                user_level=user_level,
  1235	                limit=limit,
  1236	                tier_filter=tier_filter,
  1237	                collection_override=collection_override,
  1238	                apply_filters=apply_filters,
  1239	            )
  1240	
  1241	    async def hybrid_search_with_reranking(
  1242	        self,
  1243	        query: str,
  1244	        user_level: int,
  1245	        limit: int = 5,
  1246	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] = None,
  1247	        collection_override: str | None = None,
  1248	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1249	        """
  1250	        Full hybrid search pipeline: BM25 + Dense + RRF + reranking.
  1251	
  1252	        This is the most comprehensive search method combining:
  1253	        1. Dense vector search (semantic)
  1254	        2. BM25 sparse vector search (keyword)
  1255	        3. Reciprocal Rank Fusion (combining both)
  1256	        4. CrossEncoder or Ze-Rank reranking (final precision)
  1257	
  1258	        Args:
  1259	            query: Search query
  1260	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
  1261	            limit: Max results (final count after all stages)
  1262	            tier_filter: Optional specific tier filter
  1263	            collection_override: Force specific collection
  1264	
  1265	        Returns:
  1266	            Search results with full pipeline metadata
  1267	        """
  1268	        pipeline_start_time = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1269	
  1270	        # 1. Hybrid search with overfetch for reranking
  1271	        initial_limit = limit * 3
  1272	        results = await self.hybrid_search(
  1273	            query=query,
  1274	            user_level=user_level,
  1275	            limit=initial_limit,
  1276	            tier_filter=tier_filter,
  1277	            collection_override=collection_override,
  1278	            apply_filters=True,
  1279	        )
  1280	
  1281	        # 2. Early exit for high-confidence results
  1282	        if results["results"] and results["results"][0].get("score", 0) > 0.85:
  1283	            logger.info(
  1284	                f"⚡ Early exit: Top result score {results['results'][0]['score']:.3f} > 0.85",
  1285	            )
  1286	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE:
  1287	                rag_early_exit_total.inc()
  1288	            results["results"] = results["results"][:limit]
  1289	            results["reranked"] = False
  1290	            results["early_exit"] = True
  1291	            return results
  1292	
  1293	        # 3. Re-rank with Ze-Rank 2
  1294	        reranker = self._init_reranker()
  1295	        if reranker.enabled:
  1296	            logger.info(
  1297	                f"🔍 Re-ranking {len(results['results'])} hybrid candidates for: '{query[:50]}'",
  1298	            )
  1299	            rerank_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1300	            reranked_docs = await reranker.rerank(query, results["results"], top_k=limit)
  1301	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE and rerank_start:
  1302	                rag_reranking_duration.observe(time.time() - rerank_start)
  1303	            results["results"] = reranked_docs
  1304	            results["reranked"] = True
  1305	            results["early_exit"] = False
  1306	        else:
  1307	            results["results"] = results["results"][:limit]
  1308	            results["reranked"] = False
  1309	            results["early_exit"] = False
  1310

   924	    async def get(self, ids: list[str], include: list[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   925	        """
   926	        Retrieve points by IDs (Qdrant-compatible interface).
   927	
   928	        Args:
   929	            ids: List of point IDs to retrieve
   930	            include: List of fields to include (e.g., ["embeddings", "payload"])
   931	
   932	        Returns:
   933	            Dictionary with Qdrant-compatible format
   934	        """
   935	        try:
   936	            client = await self._get_client()
   937	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points"
   938	
   939	            # Qdrant retrieve endpoint (POST /collections/{name}/points).
   940	            # BUG FIX (2026-07-19, verified live task #22): with_payload/with_vector
   941	            # are JSON BODY fields on this endpoint, NOT query params — Qdrant's
   942	            # REST API silently ignores them when sent as query params, so any
   943	            # caller passing include=[...] got 200 OK with the flags dropped
   944	            # (empty payload/vectors, scar family #2 "green but not working").
   945	            # Ref: https://api.qdrant.tech/api-reference/points/get-points
   946	            # (PointRequest schema: ids / with_payload / with_vector — singular
   947	            # "with_vector", not "with_vectors").
   948	            payload: dict[str, Any] = {"ids": ids}
   949	            if include:
   950	                # Map Qdrant-compatible include to Qdrant with_payload/with_vector
   951	                with_payload = "payload" in include or "metadatas" in include
   952	                with_vector = "embeddings" in include
   953	                if with_payload:
   954	                    payload["with_payload"] = True
   955	                if with_vector:
   956	                    payload["with_vector"] = True
   957	            else:
   958	                payload["with_payload"] = True
   959	                payload["with_vector"] = True
   960	
   961	            try:
   962	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
   963	                response.raise_for_status()
   964	
   965	                results = response.json().get("result", [])
   966	
   967	                # Transform to Qdrant format
   968	                formatted = {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   969	
   970	                for point in results:
   971	                    formatted["ids"].append(str(point["id"]))
   972	                    if "vector" in point:
   973	                        formatted["embeddings"].append(point["vector"])
   974	                    else:
   975	                        formatted["embeddings"].append(None)
   976	
   977	                    payload_data = point.get("payload", {})
   978	                    formatted["documents"].append(payload_data.get("text", ""))
   979	                    formatted["metadatas"].append(_extract_point_metadata(payload_data))
   980	
   981	                return formatted
   982	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
   983	                logger.error(f"Qdrant get failed: {e.response.status_code} - {e.response.text}")
   984	                return {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   985	            except Exception as e:
   986	                logger.error("Qdrant get request error: %s", e)
   987	                return {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   988	
   989	        except Exception as e:
   990	            logger.error("Qdrant get error: %s", e)
   991	            return {"ids": [], "embeddings": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
   992	
   993	    async def set_payload(self, ids: list[str], payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   994	        """Merge payload fields into existing points without replacing provenance."""
   995	        if not ids:
   996	            raise ValueError("ids cannot be empty")
   997	        if not payload:
   998	            raise ValueError("payload cannot be empty")
   999	
  1000	        client = await self._get_client()
  1001	        response = await client.post(
  1002	            f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/payload",
  1003	            json={"points": ids, "payload": payload},
  1004	            params={"wait": "true"},
  1005	        )
  1006	        response.raise_for_status()
  1007	        return {
  1008	            "success": True,
  1009	            "updated": len(ids),
  1010	            "collection": self.collection_name,
  1011	        }

  1013	    async def set_payload_by_filter(
  1014	        self,
  1015	        *,
  1016	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any],
  1017	        payload: dict[str, Any],
  1018	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1019	        """Atomically merge payload into every point matching a strict filter."""
  1020	        if not metadata_filter:
  1021	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter cannot be empty")
  1022	        if not payload:
  1023	            raise ValueError("payload cannot be empty")
  1024	        qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1025	            metadata_filter,
  1026	            include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1027	        )
  1028	        if not qdrant_filter:
  1029	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter did not produce a Qdrant filter")
  1030	
  1031	        client = await self._get_client()
  1032	        response = await client.post(
  1033	            f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/payload",
  1034	            json={"filter": qdrant_filter, "payload": payload},
  1035	            params={"wait": "true"},
  1036	        )
  1037	        response.raise_for_status()
  1038	        return {"success": True, "collection": self.collection_name}

  1040	    async def delete_by_filter(
  1041	        self,
  1042	        *,
  1043	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any],
  1044	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1045	        """Delete every point matching a strict metadata filter or raise."""
  1046	        if not metadata_filter:
  1047	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter cannot be empty")
  1048	        qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1049	            metadata_filter,
  1050	            include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1051	        )
  1052	        if not qdrant_filter:
  1053	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter did not produce a Qdrant filter")
  1054	
  1055	        client = await self._get_client()
  1056	        response = await client.post(
  1057	            f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/delete",
  1058	            json={"filter": qdrant_filter},
  1059	            params={"wait": "true"},
  1060	        )
  1061	        response.raise_for_status()
  1062	        return {"success": True, "collection": self.collection_name}

  1064	    async def delete(
  1065	        self,
  1066	        ids: list[str],
  1067	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1068	        """
  1069	        Delete points by IDs (Qdrant-compatible interface).
  1070	
  1071	        Args:
  1072	            ids: List of point IDs to delete
  1073	
  1074	        Returns:
  1075	            Dictionary with operation results
  1076	
  1077	        Raises:
  1078	            Exception: If delete fails
  1079	        """
  1080	        try:
  1081	            client = await self._get_client()
  1082	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/delete"
  1083	
  1084	            payload = {"points": ids}
  1085	            try:

  1087	                response.raise_for_status()

  1090	                    f"Deleted {len(ids)} points from Qdrant collection '{self.collection_name}'",
  1091	                )
  1092	                return {"success": True, "deleted_count": len(ids)}
  1093	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1094	                logger.error(f"Qdrant delete failed: {e.response.status_code} - {e.response.text}")
  1095	                return {"success": False, "error": f"HTTP {e.response.status_code}"}
  1096	            except Exception as e:

  1114	    async def scroll(
  1115	        self,
  1116	        limit: int = 10,
  1117	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1118	    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1119	        """
  1120	        Scroll through points in the collection with optional filtering.
  1121	
  1122	        Args:
  1123	            limit: Maximum number of points to return
  1124	            metadata_filter: Optional filter dict (e.g., {"agent": "main"})
  1125	
  1126	        Returns:
  1127	            List of points with id and payload: [{"id": "...", "payload": {...}}, ...]
  1128	        """
  1129	        try:
  1130	            client = await self._get_client()
  1131	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/scroll"
  1132	
  1133	            payload = {"limit": limit, "with_payload": True, "with_vectors": False}
  1134	
  1135	            # Add filter if provided
  1136	            if metadata_filter:
  1137	                qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1138	                    metadata_filter,
  1139	                    include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1140	                )
  1141	                if qdrant_filter:
  1142	                    payload["filter"] = qdrant_filter
  1143	
  1144	            try:
  1145	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1146	                response.raise_for_status()
  1147	
  1148	                data = response.json().get("result", {})
  1149	                points = data.get("points", [])
  1150	
  1151	                # Return list of points with id and payload
  1152	                return [{"id": str(p["id"]), "payload": p.get("payload", {})} for p in points]
  1153	
  1154	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1155	                logger.error(f"Qdrant scroll failed: {e.response.status_code}")
  1156	                return []
  1157	            except Exception as e:
  1158	                logger.error("Qdrant scroll request error: %s", e)
  1159	                return []
  1160	
  1161	        except Exception as e:
  1162	            logger.error("Error scrolling Qdrant collection: %s", e)
  1163	            return []

  1165	    async def scroll_strict(
  1166	        self,
  1167	        *,
  1168	        metadata_filter: dict[str, Any],
  1169	        page_size: int = 256,
  1170	        max_points: int = 10_000,
  1171	    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1172	        """Return all matching points or raise; intended for safety invariants."""
  1173	        if not metadata_filter:
  1174	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter cannot be empty")
  1175	        if page_size <= 0 or max_points <= 0:
  1176	            raise ValueError("page_size and max_points must be positive")
  1177	
  1178	        qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1179	            metadata_filter,
  1180	            include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1181	        )
  1182	        if not qdrant_filter:
  1183	            raise ValueError("metadata_filter did not produce a Qdrant filter")
  1184	
  1185	        client = await self._get_client()
  1186	        url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/scroll"
  1187	        points: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1188	        offset: Any | None = None
  1189	
  1190	        while True:
  1191	            remaining = max_points - len(points)
  1192	            if remaining <= 0:
  1193	                raise RuntimeError("Qdrant strict scroll exceeded max_points")
  1194	            payload: dict[str, Any] = {
  1195	                "limit": min(page_size, remaining),
  1196	                "with_payload": True,
  1197	                "with_vectors": False,
  1198	                "filter": qdrant_filter,
  1199	            }
  1200	            if offset is not None:
  1201	                payload["offset"] = offset
  1202	
  1203	            response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1204	            response.raise_for_status()
  1205	            result = response.json().get("result", {})
  1206	            batch = result.get("points", [])
  1207	            points.extend(
  1208	                {"id": str(point["id"]), "payload": point.get("payload", {})}
  1209	                for point in batch
  1210	            )
  1211	            offset = result.get("next_page_offset")
  1212	            if offset is None:
  1213	                return points
  1214	
  1215	    async def peek(
  1216	        self,
  1217	        limit: int = 10,
  1218	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1219	        """
  1220	        Peek at points in the collection (Qdrant-compatible interface).
  1221	
  1222	        Args:
  1223	            limit: Maximum number of points to return
  1224	
  1225	        Returns:
  1226	            Dictionary with sample points in Qdrant format
  1227	        """

  1229	            client = await self._get_client()
  1230	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/scroll"
  1231	
  1232	            payload = {"limit": limit, "with_payload": True, "with_vectors": False}
  1233	            try:
  1234	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1235	                response.raise_for_status()
  1236	
  1237	                data = response.json().get("result", {})
  1238	                points = data.get("points", [])
  1239	
  1240	                # Transform to Qdrant format
  1241	                return {
  1242	                    "ids": [str(p["id"]) for p in points],
  1243	                    "documents": [p.get("payload", {}).get("text", "") for p in points],
  1244	                    "metadatas": [_extract_point_metadata(p.get("payload", {})) for p in points],
  1245	                }
  1246	
  1247	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1248	                logger.error(f"Qdrant peek failed: {e.response.status_code}")
  1249	                return {"ids": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
  1250	            except Exception as e:
  1251	                logger.error("Qdrant peek request error: %s", e)
  1252	                return {"ids": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}
  1253	
  1254	        except Exception as e:
  1255	            logger.error("Error peeking Qdrant collection: %s", e)
  1256	            return {"ids": [], "documents": [], "metadatas": []}

  1258	    async def hybrid_search(
  1259	        self,
  1260	        query_embedding: list[float],
  1261	        query_sparse: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1262	        filter: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1263	        limit: int = 5,
  1264	        prefetch_limit: int = 20,
  1265	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1266	        """
  1267	        Hybrid search combining dense and sparse (BM25) vectors with RRF fusion.
  1268	
  1269	        Uses Qdrant's prefetch + fusion API for optimal hybrid search.
  1270	
  1271	        Args:
  1272	            query_embedding: Dense query embedding vector (1536 dims)
  1273	            query_sparse: Sparse query vector {"indices": [...], "values": [...]}
  1274	            filter: Metadata filter (simplified format)
  1275	            limit: Maximum number of final results
  1276	            prefetch_limit: Number of candidates to prefetch from each vector type
  1277	
  1278	        Returns:
  1279	            Dictionary with search results in standard format
  1280	        """
  1281	        # If no sparse vector provided, fall back to dense-only search
  1282	        if not query_sparse or not query_sparse.get("indices"):
  1283	            return await self.search(query_embedding, filter=filter, limit=limit)
  1284	
  1285	        async def _do_hybrid_search():
  1286	            client = await self._get_client()
  1287	            url = f"/collections/{self.collection_name}/points/query"
  1288	
  1289	            # Build prefetch queries for both dense and sparse
  1290	            prefetch = [
  1291	                {
  1292	                    "query": query_embedding,
  1293	                    "using": "dense",
  1294	                    "limit": prefetch_limit,
  1295	                },
  1296	                {
  1297	                    "query": {
  1298	                        "indices": query_sparse["indices"],
  1299	                        "values": query_sparse["values"],
  1300	                    },
  1301	                    "using": "bm25",
  1302	                    "limit": prefetch_limit,
  1303	                },
  1304	            ]
  1305	
  1306	            # Main query with RRF fusion
  1307	            payload = {
  1308	                "prefetch": prefetch,
  1309	                "query": {"fusion": "rrf"},  # Reciprocal Rank Fusion
  1310	                "limit": limit,
  1311	                "with_payload": True,
  1312	            }
  1313	
  1314	            # Add filter if provided
  1315	            if filter:
  1316	                qdrant_filter = self._convert_filter_to_qdrant_format(
  1317	                    filter,
  1318	                    include_flat_payload=self._include_flat_payload_filters(),
  1319	                )
  1320	                if qdrant_filter:
  1321	                    payload["filter"] = qdrant_filter
  1322	
  1323	            try:
  1324	                response = await client.post(url, json=payload)
  1325	                response.raise_for_status()
  1326	
  1327	                data = response.json()
  1328	                results = data.get("result", {}).get("points", [])
  1329	
  1330	                # Transform results to standard format
  1331	                formatted_results = {
  1332	                    "ids": [str(r["id"]) for r in results],
  1333	                    "documents": [r["payload"].get("text", "") for r in results],
  1334	                    "metadatas": [_extract_point_metadata(r["payload"]) for r in results],
  1335	                    "distances": [1.0 - r.get("score", 0) for r in results],
  1336	                    "scores": [r.get("score", 0) for r in results],
  1337	                    "total_found": len(results),
  1338	                    "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
  1339	                }
  1340	
  1341	                logger.debug(
  1342	                    f"Hybrid search: collection={self.collection_name}, "
  1343	                    f"found {len(results)} results (RRF fusion)",
  1344	                )
  1345	                return formatted_results
  1346	
  1347	            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
  1348	                error_text = e.response.text if hasattr(e.response, "text") else str(e.response)
  1349	                error_lower = error_text.lower()
  1350	                # If hybrid search fails (e.g., collection doesn't have sparse/named vectors),
  1351	                # fall back to dense-only search
  1352	                # Covers: "sparse" errors, "dense" errors, "Vector params" errors
  1353	                if e.response.status_code == 400 and (
  1354	                    "sparse" in error_lower
  1355	                    or "dense" in error_lower
  1356	                    or "vector params" in error_lower
  1357	                ):
  1358	                    logger.warning(
  1359	                        f"Hybrid search not available for {self.collection_name}, "
  1360	                        "falling back to dense search",
  1361	                    )
  1362	                    return await self.search(query_embedding, filter=filter, limit=limit)
  1363	                logger.error(
  1364	                    f"Qdrant hybrid search failed: {e.response.status_code} - {error_text}",
  1365	                )
  1366	                return {
  1367	                    "ids": [],
  1368	                    "documents": [],
  1369	                    "metadatas": [],
  1370	                    "distances": [],
  1371	                    "scores": [],
  1372	                    "total_found": 0,
  1373	                    "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
  1374	                    "error": error_text,
  1375	                }
  1376	            except Exception as e:
  1377	                logger.error("Qdrant hybrid search request error: %s", e)
  1378	                raise QdrantConnectionError(f"Qdrant connection error: {e}") from e
  1379	
  1380	        start_time = time.time()
  1381	        # 🔍 TRACING: Span for Qdrant hybrid search
  1382	        with trace_span(
  1383	            "qdrant.hybrid_search",
  1384	            {
  1385	                "collection": self.collection_name,
  1386	                "limit": limit,
  1387	                "prefetch_limit": prefetch_limit,
  1388	                "has_filter": bool(filter),
  1389	                "search_type": "hybrid_rrf",
  1390	            },
  1391	        ):
  1392	            try:
  1393	                result = await _retry_with_backoff(_do_hybrid_search)
  1394	                elapsed = time.time() - start_time
  1395	                _qdrant_metrics["search_calls"] += 1
  1396	                _qdrant_metrics["search_total_time"] += elapsed
  1397	                set_span_attribute("results_count", result.get("total_found", 0))
  1398	                set_span_attribute("latency_ms", int(elapsed * 1000))
  1399	                set_span_status("ok")
  1400	                logger.debug(f"Qdrant hybrid search completed in {elapsed * 1000:.2f}ms")

  1321	                    payload["filter"] = qdrant_filter

  1241	    async def hybrid_search_with_reranking(
  1242	        self,
  1243	        query: str,
  1244	        user_level: int,
  1245	        limit: int = 5,
  1246	        tier_filter: list[TierLevel] = None,
  1247	        collection_override: str | None = None,
  1248	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1249	        """
  1250	        Full hybrid search pipeline: BM25 + Dense + RRF + reranking.
  1251	
  1252	        This is the most comprehensive search method combining:
  1253	        1. Dense vector search (semantic)
  1254	        2. BM25 sparse vector search (keyword)
  1255	        3. Reciprocal Rank Fusion (combining both)
  1256	        4. CrossEncoder or Ze-Rank reranking (final precision)
  1257	
  1258	        Args:
  1259	            query: Search query
  1260	            user_level: User access level (0-3)
  1261	            limit: Max results (final count after all stages)
  1262	            tier_filter: Optional specific tier filter
  1263	            collection_override: Force specific collection
  1264	
  1265	        Returns:
  1266	            Search results with full pipeline metadata
  1267	        """
  1268	        pipeline_start_time = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1269	
  1270	        # 1. Hybrid search with overfetch for reranking
  1271	        initial_limit = limit * 3
  1272	        results = await self.hybrid_search(
  1273	            query=query,
  1274	            user_level=user_level,
  1275	            limit=initial_limit,
  1276	            tier_filter=tier_filter,
  1277	            collection_override=collection_override,
  1278	            apply_filters=True,
  1279	        )
  1280	
  1281	        # 2. Early exit for high-confidence results
  1282	        if results["results"] and results["results"][0].get("score", 0) > 0.85:
  1283	            logger.info(
  1284	                f"⚡ Early exit: Top result score {results['results'][0]['score']:.3f} > 0.85",
  1285	            )
  1286	            if METRICS_AVAILABLE:
  1287	                rag_early_exit_total.inc()
  1288	            results["results"] = results["results"][:limit]
  1289	            results["reranked"] = False
  1290	            results["early_exit"] = True
  1291	            return results
  1292	
  1293	        # 3. Re-rank with Ze-Rank 2
  1294	        reranker = self._init_reranker()
  1295	        if reranker.enabled:
  1296	            logger.info(
  1297	                f"🔍 Re-ranking {len(results['results'])} hybrid candidates for: '{query[:50]}'",
  1298	            )
  1299	            rerank_start = time.time() if METRICS_AVAILABLE else None
  1300	            reranked_docs = await reranker.rerank(query, results["results"], top_k=limit)

   289	# raw_results carries no `search_type` is labelled dense_formatted even
   290	# though search_service.py:1142 still calls it "hybrid_rrf". The two
   291	# disagree ON PURPOSE (R1) — score_kind comes from the caller's own branch
   292	# (checked via the key's PRESENCE, never its value), the search_type lie is
   293	# B2's to fix. On origin/main `score_kind` does not exist on a result dict
   294	# at all, so this assertion cannot even be expressed there.
   295	# ============================================================================

   297	
   298	@pytest.mark.asyncio
   299	async def test_09_dense_fallback_without_search_type_key_labelled_dense_formatted(
   300	    search_service: SearchService,
   301	) -> None:
   302	    async def fake_hybrid_search(*, query_embedding, query_sparse, filter, limit, prefetch_limit):
   303	        # Simulates core/qdrant_db.py:1362's internal fallback: a

   306	            "ids": ["1"],
   307	            "documents": ["doc"],
   308	            "metadatas": [{}],
   309	            "distances": [0.1],

   337	
   338	def _formatter_backed_retriever() -> FakeRetriever:
   339	    """A retriever whose hits are minted by the REAL formatter.
   340	
   341	    Case 10 is a CROSSING test, so its inputs may not be hand-written dicts
   342	    carrying a hand-written `score_kind`: that would assert the test's own
   343	    imagination rather than the chain. These hits are produced by calling
   344	    `format_search_results` exactly as `SearchService.hybrid_search` calls it
   345	    on the hybrid branch — the same function, the same declared kind — so what
   346	    the package builder receives here has the shape production hands it.
   347	
   348	    The first draft of case 10 used the shared `_visa_retriever()` fake, whose
   349	    hits declare no kind at all. It passed on key-presence alone while every
   350	    source reached the scorer as UNKNOWN, which is the precise failure the
   351	    strengthened assertion below exists to catch.
   352	    """
   353	    raw = {
   354	        "ids": ["1", "2"],
   355	        "documents": [
   356	            "KITAS requires a sponsor letter and passport copy.",
   357	            "Immigration law UU 6/2011 governs stay permits.",
   358	        ],
   359	        "metadatas": [{}, {}],
   360	        # 1/(2+0) and 1/(2+1): first and second of ONE contributing list,
   361	        # from the measured server-side RRF table.
   362	        "distances": [1.0 - 0.5, 1.0 - (1.0 / 3.0)],
   363	        "scores": [0.5, 1.0 / 3.0],
   364	        "total_found": 2,
   365	    }
   366	    formatted = format_search_results(
   367	        raw,
   368	        "visa_oracle",
   369	        primary_collection=None,
   370	        query=VISA_QUERY,
   371	        score_kind=score_provenance.HYBRID_RRF_FORMATTED,
   372	    )
   373	    return FakeRetriever({"visa_oracle": formatted})
   374

   376	# ============================================================================
   377	# Case 10 — PROJECTION test (staff-room requirement 2): the list handed to
   378	# calculate_evidence_score at wa_package_builder.py:594-598 carries
   379	# score_kind on EVERY element. Fails if the projection is ever re-narrowed
   380	# to {"score": ...} only.
   381	# ============================================================================
   382	
   383	
   384	@pytest.mark.asyncio
   385	async def test_10_scorer_projection_carries_score_kind_on_every_element(
   386	    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
   387	) -> None:
   388	    captured: dict[str, list] = {}
   389	
   390	    def fake_calculate_evidence_score(*, sources, context_gathered, query):
   391	        captured["sources"] = sources
   392	        return 0.5
   393	
   394	    monkeypatch.setattr(wpb_module, "calculate_evidence_score", fake_calculate_evidence_score)
   395	
   396	    await wpb_module.build_context_package(
   397	        query=VISA_QUERY,
   398	        history=[],
   399	        thread_epoch=0,
   400	        retriever=_formatter_backed_retriever(),
   401	    )
   402	
   403	    assert captured.get("sources"), "expected at least one source handed to the scorer"
   404	    for source in captured["sources"]:
   405	        assert score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY in source
   406	    # Presence alone is not enough: stamping every element UNKNOWN would also
   407	    # satisfy the loop above while destroying the contract's whole point. At
   408	    # least one element must carry a kind that a WRITER actually declared.
   409	    declared = {
   410	        source[score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY] for source in captured["sources"]
   411	    } - {score_provenance.UNKNOWN}
   412	    assert declared, (
   413	        "every source reached the scorer as UNKNOWN — the projection kept the key "
   414	        "but the chain lost the provenance somewhere upstream"
   415	    )

   444	# ============================================================================
   445	# Case 12 (addition, R2 backward-compat condition — staff-room ack
   446	# 2026-09-10T20:54:08Z, reiterated by the Dux): `package_hash` is
   447	# persisted and RE-VERIFIED ACROSS THE DEPLOY BOUNDARY
   448	# (services/integrations/wa_broker.py:468/691/885,
   449	# app/routers/wa_broker.py:279, app/routers/wa_package.py:161,
   450	# services/integrations/wa_codex_daemon.py:342) — so the question is not
   451	# "does the new hash differ" but "does a package sealed BEFORE this PR
   452	# still verify after it". `_canonical_wire`/`_package_hash` have no
   453	# per-field allowlist (spec R2): they are content-agnostic
   454	# `json.dumps(sort_keys=True)` over whatever they are given, so a stored
   455	# dict lacking score_kind/score_raw must re-hash to its own original value,
   456	# unchanged.
   457	# ============================================================================
   458	
   459	
   460	def test_a_package_sealed_before_score_kind_existed_still_verifies() -> None:
   461	    """A pre-PR sealed package — chunks with NO score_kind/score_raw key at
   462	    all — must still verify under the NEW code.
   463	
   464	    The proof is `ContextPackage.__post_init__` (wa_package_builder.py
   465	    :153-170) itself NOT raising on construction: it recomputes
   466	    `_canonical_wire` over the given fields and raises ValueError if the
   467	    digest doesn't match `package_hash`. That IS the real verification the
   468	    deploy boundary relies on when it reloads a stored package — this test
   469	    exercises that exact path rather than re-implementing the hash check.
   470	    """
   471	    history = [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}]
   472	    chunks = [{"collection": "visa_oracle", "text": "legacy chunk, no score_kind", "score": 0.7}]
   473	    assert score_provenance.SCORE_KIND_KEY not in chunks[0]  # the pre-PR shape, made explicit
   474	    pricing_block = None
   475	    persona_digest = "pd"
   476	    evidence_inputs = {
   477	        "evidence_score": 0.5,
   478	        "context_length": 1,
   479	        "domain": "visa",
   480	        "label_threshold": 0.12,
   481	        "abstain": False,
   482	        "dlp": False,
   483	    }
   484	    thread_epoch = 1
   485	
   486	    # The digest a pre-PR build would have sealed — computed once, exactly
   487	    # as the original build did, never recomputed again below.
   488	    package_hash = wpb_module._package_hash(
   489	        history=history,
   490	        chunks=chunks,
   491	        pricing_block=pricing_block,
   492	        persona_digest=persona_digest,
   493	        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,
   494	        thread_epoch=thread_epoch,
   495	    )
   496	
   497	    # __post_init__ re-verifies digest == wire; constructing without a
   498	    # raised ValueError IS the proof that the pre-PR digest still holds.
   499	    package = ContextPackage(
   500	        history=history,
   501	        chunks=chunks,
   502	        pricing_block=pricing_block,
   503	        persona_digest=persona_digest,
   504	        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,
   505	        thread_epoch=thread_epoch,
   506	        package_hash=package_hash,
   507	    )
   508	    assert package.package_hash == package_hash

    52	class LRUCache:
    53	    """
    54	    LRU Cache with TTL support for in-memory fallback.
    55	    Automatically evicts least recently used items when max size reached.
    56	    """
    57	
    58	    def __init__(
    59	        self,
    60	        max_size: int = DEFAULT_MAX_MEMORY_CACHE_SIZE,
    61	        maxsize: int | None = None,  # Alias for max_size (for compatibility)
    62	        default_ttl: int = DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL,
    63	    ) -> None:
    64	        """
    65	        Initialize LRU cache.
    66	
    67	        Args:
    68	            max_size: Maximum number of items to store
    69	            maxsize: Alias for max_size (for compatibility with tests)
    70	            default_ttl: Default TTL in seconds
    71	        """
    72	        self.max_size = maxsize if maxsize is not None else max_size
    73	        self.default_ttl = default_ttl
    74	        self.cache: OrderedDict[str, tuple[Any, float]] = (
    75	            OrderedDict()
    76	        )  # key -> (value, expire_time)
    77	
    78	    def get(self, key: str) -> Any | None:
    79	        """Get value from cache if not expired."""
    80	        if key not in self.cache:
    81	            return None
    82	
    83	        value, expire_time = self.cache[key]
    84	
    85	        # Check if expired
    86	        if time.time() > expire_time:
    87	            del self.cache[key]
    88	            return None
    89	
    90	        # Move to end (most recently used)
    91	        self.cache.move_to_end(key)
    92	        return value
    93	
    94	    def set(self, key: str, value: Any, ttl: int | None = None) -> None:
    95	        """Set value in cache with TTL."""
    96	        ttl = ttl if ttl is not None else self.default_ttl
    97	        expire_time = time.time() + ttl
    98	
    99	        if key in self.cache:
   100	            # Update existing
   101	            self.cache.move_to_end(key)
   102	        else:
   103	            # Check if we need to evict
   104	            if len(self.cache) >= self.max_size:
   105	                # Remove least recently used (first item)
   106	                self.cache.popitem(last=False)
   107	
   108	        self.cache[key] = (value, expire_time)
   109	
   110	    def delete(self, key: str) -> bool:
   111	        """Delete key from cache."""
   112	        if key in self.cache:
   113	            del self.cache[key]
   114	            return True
   115	        return False
   116	
   117	    def clear(self) -> int:
   118	        """Clear all cache entries."""
   119	        count = len(self.cache)
   120	        self.cache.clear()
   210	
   211	        filtered_kwargs = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if self._is_serializable(v)}
   212	
   213	        key_data = json.dumps(
   214	            {"args": filtered_args, "kwargs": filtered_kwargs},
   215	            sort_keys=True,
   216	            default=str,
   217	        )
   218	        key_hash = hashlib.md5(key_data.encode()).hexdigest()[:CACHE_KEY_HASH_LENGTH]
   219	        return f"zantara:{prefix}:{key_hash}"
   220	
   221	    async def get(self, key: str) -> Any | None:
   222	        """Get value from cache"""
   223	        self._try_connect_redis()
   224	        try:
   225	            if self.redis_available and self.redis_client:
   226	                value = await self.redis_client.get(key)
   227	                if value:
   228	                    self.stats["hits"] += 1
   229	                    return json.loads(value)
   230	                self.stats["misses"] += 1
   231	                return None
   232	            value = self._memory_cache.get(key)
   233	            if value is not None:
   234	                self.stats["hits"] += 1
   235	                return value
   236	            self.stats["misses"] += 1
   237	            return None
   238	        except Exception as e:
   239	            logger.error("Cache get error: %s", e)
   240	            self.stats["errors"] += 1
   241	            return None
   242	
   243	    async def set(self, key: str, value: Any, ttl: int | None = None) -> bool:
   244	        """Set value in cache with TTL (seconds). Uses RedisManager TTL strategy if ttl not provided."""
   245	        self._try_connect_redis()
   246	        if ttl is None:
   247	            # Extract prefix from key format "zantara:{prefix}:{hash}"
   248	            from backend.core.redis_manager import get_ttl
   249	
   250	            parts = key.split(":")
   251	            prefix = parts[1] if len(parts) >= 3 else "default"
   252	            ttl = get_ttl(prefix)
   253	        try:
   254	            if self.redis_available and self.redis_client:
   255	                await self.redis_client.setex(key, ttl, json.dumps(value, cls=DecimalEncoder))
   256	                return True
   257	            self._memory_cache.set(key, value, ttl)
   258	            return True
   259	        except Exception as e:
   260	            logger.error("Cache set error: %s", e)
   261	            self.stats["errors"] += 1
   262	            return False
   263	
   264	    async def delete(self, key: str) -> bool:
   265	        """Delete key from cache"""
    85	)

    87	# Fixed, documented access level for this deterministic, unauthenticated WA
    88	# path — matches `VectorSearchTool.__init__`'s own default (tools.py:89),
    89	# the closest existing analog for "no elevated tier, anonymous caller".
    90	_SEARCH_USER_LEVEL = 1

    92	# Persona digest: a MINIMAL, deterministic set of the existing SSOT prompt
    93	# sections from backend/prompts/zantara_core.py (the sole prompt SSOT per
    94	# apps/backend-rag/CLAUDE.md "Prompt Architecture") — chosen because they
    95	# most directly constrain HOW a WA reply gets written (language selection,
    96	# citation discipline, greeting handling). Deliberately excluded: the
    97	# creator/team personas, the master template, and the internal-monologue /
    98	# escalation / crash-protocol sections — those are orchestration-only
    99	# (deciding what tools to call, how to escalate) and irrelevant to a
   100	# downstream text generator that receives an already-built evidence
   101	# package and just has to write the reply.
   102	_PERSONA_SECTIONS: tuple[str, ...] = (LANGUAGE_PROTOCOL, CITATION_RULES, GREETING_RULES)

   104	
   105	class PackageUnbuildable(Exception):
   106	    """The deterministic pipeline cannot build a package for this query.
   107	
   108	    The caller (the wa_package router) treats this as a ROUTE decision, not
   109	    an error: the whole row goes to the Gemini leg, never an LLM-borrowed
   110	    fallback inside the codex path (spec §2.2).
   111	    """
   112	
   113	    def __init__(self, reason: str) -> None:
   114	        self.reason = reason
   115	        super().__init__(reason)
   116	
   117	
   118	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   119	class ContextPackage:
   120	    """The deterministic context package for the codex-route WA generation leg.
   121	
   122	    Allowlist schema (spec §2.2) — EXACTLY these 7 fields serialize via
   123	    `to_payload()`. Adding an 8th field to this dataclass does NOT
   124	    automatically leak it into the wire payload: `to_payload()` builds the
   125	    dict literally (never `dataclasses.asdict()` plus extras), so the
   126	    allowlist is enforced by construction, not by convention.
   127	    """
   128	
   129	    history: list[dict[str, str]]
   130	    chunks: list[dict[str, Any]]
   131	    pricing_block: dict[str, Any] | None
   132	    persona_digest: str
   133	    evidence_inputs: dict[str, Any]
   134	    thread_epoch: int
   135	    package_hash: str
   136	    # DLP reversal map (G-P3), placeholder -> original PII value. Populated
   137	    # ONLY when `build_context_package(dlp=True)` redacted this package's
   138	    # history/chunks; empty otherwise. Deliberately EXCLUDED from
   139	    # `_canonical_wire`/`__post_init__`'s hash domain, from `to_payload()`
   140	    # and from `wire_text()` — it is not part of the 7-key allowlist and
   141	    # must never enter the wire package, the package_hash inputs, or a log
   142	    # line (spec: "never leaves Fly"). `repr=False` so an accidental
   143	    # `logger.debug(package)`/`repr(package)` cannot print original PII.
   144	    reversal_map: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict, repr=False, compare=False)
   145	    # Wire snapshot, sealed at construction (Codex S2 re-verdict r6,
   146	    # finding 2): `frozen=True` freezes the field BINDINGS only — the
   147	    # nested lists/dicts stay mutable, so serializing them lazily would let
   148	    # `pkg.history[-1]["content"] = ...` between build and offer divorce
   149	    # the wire bytes from package_hash. Serializing ONCE here, and
   150	    # verifying the hash against the snapshot in __post_init__, makes the
   151	    # pair immutable together for the object's whole life.
   152	    _wire: str = field(init=False, repr=False, compare=False, default="")
   153	
   154	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   155	        wire = _canonical_wire(
   156	            {
   157	                "history": self.history,
   158	                "chunks": self.chunks,
   159	                "pricing_block": self.pricing_block,
   160	                "persona_digest": self.persona_digest,
   161	                "evidence_inputs": self.evidence_inputs,
   162	                "thread_epoch": self.thread_epoch,
   163	            }
   164	        )
   165	        digest = hashlib.sha256(wire.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   166	        if digest != self.package_hash:
   167	            raise ValueError(
   168	                "package_hash does not cover this package's wire bytes "
   169	                "(hash/wire integrity is sealed at construction)"
   170	            )
   171	        object.__setattr__(self, "_wire", wire)
   172	
   173	    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   174	        """Serialize to the exact 7-key allowlist. No extras, ever.
   175	
   176	        Returns a DEEP COPY (S2 cross-family review, finding 5):
   177	        `frozen=True` freezes the field BINDINGS, not the mutable
   178	        lists/dicts inside them — handing out the internal references
   179	        would let a caller mutate content after `package_hash` was
   180	        computed, silently divorcing the payload from its own hash.
   181	
   182	        NOT the broker wire format: this 7-key view INCLUDES package_hash,
   183	        so its serialization can never equal the bytes the hash covers —
   184	        offering `json.dumps(to_payload())` as broker_jobs.package makes
   185	        every broker-side hash verification fail by construction (Codex S2
   186	        re-verdict r5, finding 1). The envelope the broker receives and
   187	        verifies is ``wire_text()``; the hash travels beside it.
   188	        """
   189	        return copy.deepcopy(
   190	            {
   191	                "history": self.history,
   192	                "chunks": self.chunks,
   193	                "pricing_block": self.pricing_block,
   194	                "persona_digest": self.persona_digest,
   195	                "evidence_inputs": self.evidence_inputs,
   196	                "thread_epoch": self.thread_epoch,
   197	                "package_hash": self.package_hash,
   198	            }
   199	        )
   200	
   201	    def wire_text(self) -> str:
   202	        """The EXACT bytes offered to the broker as broker_jobs.package —
   203	        and, verbatim, the bytes ``package_hash`` covers.
   204	
   205	        One serializer (``_canonical_wire``) produces both this string and
   425	            truncated_count,
   426	            _MAX_CHUNK_CHARS,
   427	        )
   428	    return capped[:_MAX_CHUNKS]
   429	
   430	
   431	def _canonical_wire(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   432	    """The ONE serializer for the broker envelope — used by BOTH
   433	    ``ContextPackage.wire_text()`` and ``_package_hash``, so the bytes on
   434	    the wire and the bytes under the digest are the same function's output
   435	    by construction (Codex S2 re-verdict r5, finding 1). `sort_keys=True`
   436	    makes it independent of dict insertion order; compact `separators` plus
   437	    `ensure_ascii=False` keep it deterministic for identical content."""
   438	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
   439	
   440	
   441	def _package_hash(
   442	    history: list[dict[str, str]],
   443	    chunks: list[dict[str, Any]],
   444	    pricing_block: dict[str, Any] | None,
   445	    persona_digest: str,
   446	    evidence_inputs: dict[str, Any],
   447	    thread_epoch: int,
   448	) -> str:
   449	    """Content-addressed sha256 hex digest over the 6 non-hash fields —
   450	    digested over exactly ``_canonical_wire`` of them, i.e. exactly the
   451	    string ``wire_text()`` later emits."""
   452	    payload = {
   453	        "history": history,
   454	        "chunks": chunks,
   455	        "pricing_block": pricing_block,
   456	        "persona_digest": persona_digest,
   457	        "evidence_inputs": evidence_inputs,
   458	        "thread_epoch": thread_epoch,
   459	    }
   460	    return hashlib.sha256(_canonical_wire(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   461	
   462	
   463	async def build_context_package(
   464	    *,
   465	    query: str,
   550	    retrieved.sort(key=lambda c: (-float(c["score"]), c["collection"], c["text"]))
   551	    chunks.extend(retrieved)
   552	
   553	    chunks = _cap_chunks(chunks)
   554	
   555	    pricing_block: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   556	    if has_pricing_intent(query):
   557	        pricing_block = _sanitize_pricing_block(get_pricing_service().search_service(query))
   558	
   559	    payload_history = _sanitize_history(history, query)
   560	
   561	    # G-P3 DLP gate — runs AFTER chunks are capped and history sanitized,
   562	    # BEFORE evidence_inputs/package_hash are computed: everything hashed
   563	    # and sealed below is the REDACTED content, never the original (spec
   564	    # rule: "package_hash computed over the REDACTED content"). Fail-closed
   565	    # by construction — ANY exception from the redact step (detector bug,
   566	    # or wa_dlp's own >MAX_PLACEHOLDERS overflow guard) becomes
   567	    # PackageUnbuildable so the row falls off to the Gemini leg instead of
   568	    # shipping a partially-redacted or unredacted package.
   569	    #
   570	    # M1 (Kimi adversarial review, verified): `pricing_block["search_query"]`
   571	    # is the customer's RAW query, verbatim (pricing_service.py sets it from
   572	    # the same `query` argument `_sanitize_pricing_block` keeps untouched) —
   573	    # without threading it through the SAME redaction state as history, a
   574	    # NIK in the query would be redacted in `history[-1]["content"]` but
   575	    # ship unredacted a few keys over in `pricing_block.search_query`, into
   576	    # both the wire and the hash. Passed into `redact_package_fields` so it
   577	    # shares dedup/placeholder-numbering with the history copy of the same
   578	    # string, then spliced back before hashing.
   579	    reversal_map: dict[str, str] = {}
   580	    if dlp:
   581	        pricing_search_query = (
   582	            pricing_block.get("search_query") if pricing_block is not None else None
   583	        )
   584	        try:
   585	            dlp_result = redact_package_fields(payload_history, chunks, pricing_search_query)
   586	        except Exception as exc:
   587	            logger.info(
   588	                "wa_package_builder: dlp redaction failed error=%s",
   589	                type(exc).__name__,
   590	            )
   591	            raise PackageUnbuildable(reason="dlp_error") from exc
   592	        payload_history = dlp_result.history
   593	        chunks = dlp_result.chunks
   594	        reversal_map = dlp_result.reversal_map
   595	        if pricing_block is not None:
   596	            pricing_block = {**pricing_block, "search_query": dlp_result.search_query}
   597	
   598	    persona_digest = _build_persona_digest()
   599	
   600	    # Evidence inputs are FROZEN here — finalization (spec §2.3) reads these
   601	    # fields, it never recomputes them against a possibly-drifted retrieval.
   602	    abstain_policy = build_abstain_policy(query)
   603	    evidence_score = calculate_evidence_score(
   604	        sources=[
   605	            {"score": chunk["score"], "score_kind": score_provenance.kind_of(chunk)}
   606	            for chunk in chunks
   607	        ],
   608	        context_gathered=[chunk["text"] for chunk in chunks],
   609	        query=query,
   610	    )
   611	    evidence_inputs: dict[str, Any] = {
   612	        "evidence_score": evidence_score,
   613	        "context_length": len(chunks),
   614	        "domain": plan.domain.value,
   615	        "label_threshold": abstain_policy.label_threshold,
   616	        "abstain": abstain_policy.label_abstains(evidence_score),
   617	        # Kimi-7 (MINOR): inside the hash domain by design — a future
   618	        # caller that forgets `dlp=True` produces a package distinguishable
   619	        # downstream (evidence_inputs is what finalize/consumers read out
   620	        # of the sealed wire) instead of one indistinguishable from a
   621	        # properly-redacted build.
   622	        "dlp": dlp,
   623	    }
   624	
   625	    package_hash = _package_hash(
   626	        history=payload_history,
   627	        chunks=chunks,
   628	        pricing_block=pricing_block,
   629	        persona_digest=persona_digest,
   630	        evidence_inputs=evidence_inputs,

   896	class CompleteStatus(str, enum.Enum):
   897	    ACCEPTED = "accepted"  # -> completed_pending_consume (or failed, typed)
   898	    REPLAY = "replay"  # same completion_key re-POST -> same 200
   899	    CONFLICT = "conflict"  # different payload for a completed job -> 409
   900	    GONE = "gone"  # no such leased job under this fence / expired -> 410
   260	    dependencies=[Depends(require_wa_broker_key)],
   261	)
   262	async def claim(
   263	    request: Request,
   264	    pool: asyncpg.Pool = Depends(get_database_pool),
   265	) -> ClaimResponse:
   266	    body = await _read_bounded_body(request, ClaimRequest)
   267	    async with pool.acquire() as conn:
   268	        row = await wa_broker.claim_job(
   269	            conn,
   270	            in_flight=body.in_flight,
   271	            last_exec_ms=body.last_exec_ms,
   272	        )
   273	    if row is None:
   274	        return ClaimResponse()
   275	    return ClaimResponse(
   276	        job_id=row["job_id"],
   277	        fence_token=row["fence_token"],
   278	        package=row["package"],
   279	        package_hash=row["package_hash"],
   280	        deadline_at=row["deadline_at"].isoformat(),
   281	        server_now=row["server_now"].isoformat(),
   282	    )

   209	class _Claim:
   210	    job_id: str

   217	
   218	class WaCodexDaemon:
   219	    """Single-flight claim → exec → complete loop against the WA broker."""
   220	
   221	    def __init__(
   222	        self,
   223	        config: DaemonConfig,
   224	        *,
   225	        codex_client: CodexExecClient | None = None,
   226	        http_client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
   227	        transport: httpx.AsyncBaseTransport | None = None,
   228	    ) -> None:
   229	        self._config = config
   230	        self._codex = codex_client or CodexExecClient(
   320	            )
   321	        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
   322	            logger.warning("wa-codex-daemon: claim transport error: %s", type(exc).__name__)
   323	            return None
   324	        if response.status_code != 200:
   325	            logger.warning("wa-codex-daemon: claim HTTP %s", response.status_code)
   326	            return None
   327	        try:
   328	            data = response.json()
   329	        except ValueError:
   330	            # A 200 whose body is not JSON (an LB error page, a truncated
   331	            # response) is a transport blip, not a loop crash — treat it
   332	            # like any failed claim (Kimi round-1 named test gap).
   333	            logger.warning("wa-codex-daemon: claim returned non-JSON 200")
   334	            return None
   335	        if not data.get("job_id"):
   336	            return None
   337	        try:
   338	            return _Claim(
   339	                job_id=str(data["job_id"]),
   340	                fence_token=str(data["fence_token"]),
   341	                package=str(data["package"]),
   342	                package_hash=str(data["package_hash"]),
   343	                deadline_at=str(data["deadline_at"]),
   344	                server_now=str(data["server_now"]),
   345	            )
   346	        except KeyError as exc:
   347	            # A job_id with missing siblings is a contract break — there is
   348	            # no fence to complete under, so nothing can be reported; the
   349	            # lease reaper folds it server-side at deadline.
   350	            logger.error("wa-codex-daemon: claim response missing field %s", exc)
   351	            return None
   352

   142	    async def execute(self, query: str, collection: str = None, top_k: int = 8, **kwargs) -> str:
   143	        """
   144	        Execute vector search.
   145	
   146	        If collection is specified: search only that collection.
   147	        If collection is None: federated search across ALL collections.
   148	        """
   149	        with trace_span(
   150	            "tool.vector_search",
   151	            {
   152	                "query_length": len(query),
   153	                "requested_collection": collection or "federated_all",
   154	                "top_k": top_k,
   155	            },
   156	        ):
   157	            top_k = int(top_k) if top_k else 8
   158	
   159	            # Determine which collections to search
   160	            if collection:
   161	                # LLM specified a collection - trust its judgment
   162	                target_collections = [collection]
   163	                logger.info("🔍 [Vector Search] LLM selected collection: %s", collection)
   164	            else:
   165	                # No collection specified - search ALL (federated)
   166	                target_collections = AVAILABLE_COLLECTIONS.copy()
   167	                logger.info(
   168	                    f"🌐 [Federated Search] Searching all {len(target_collections)} collections",
   169	                )
   170	
   171	            set_span_attribute("collections_searched", str(target_collections))
   172	
   173	            all_chunks = []
   174	            seen_content = set()
   175	
   176	            # Execute search across target collections in parallel for better performance
   177	            import asyncio
   178	
   179	            # Determine search method based on feature flags
   180	            from backend.app.core.config import settings as _settings
   181	
   182	            use_hybrid = getattr(_settings, "enable_hybrid_search", False)
   183	
   184	            async def _search_collection(target_col) -> Any:
   185	                try:
   186	                    per_col_limit = 5 if len(target_collections) > 1 else top_k
   187	                    # Per-collection timeout to prevent one slow collection from blocking everything
   188	                    async with asyncio.timeout(15.0 if use_hybrid else 10.0):
   189	                        if use_hybrid and hasattr(self.retriever, "hybrid_search_with_reranking"):
   190	                            # Full pipeline: BM25 + Dense + RRF + CrossEncoder reranking
   191	                            res = await self.retriever.hybrid_search_with_reranking(
   192	                                query=query,
   193	                                user_level=self.user_level,
   194	                                limit=per_col_limit,
   195	                                collection_override=target_col,
   196	                            )
   197	                        elif hasattr(self.retriever, "search_with_reranking"):
   198	                            res = await self.retriever.search_with_reranking(
   199	                                query=query,
   200	                                user_level=self.user_level,
   201	                                limit=per_col_limit,
   202	                                collection_override=target_col,
   203	                            )
   204	                        else:
   205	                            res = await self.retriever.search(
   206	                                query=query,
   207	                                user_level=self.user_level,
   208	                                limit=per_col_limit,
   209	                                collection_override=target_col,
   210	                            )
   211	                        return target_col, res.get("results", [])
   212	                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   213	                    logger.warning("⏱️ Search timeout for collection %s", target_col)
   214	                    return target_col, []
   215	                except Exception as e:
   216	                    logger.warning("Search failed for %s: %s", target_col, e)

   218	
   219	            if use_hybrid:
   220	                logger.info("🔀 [Hybrid Search] Using BM25+Dense+RRF+CrossEncoder pipeline")
   221	
   222	            # Structured concurrency (Python 3.11+); _search_collection swallows exceptions.
   223	            async with asyncio.TaskGroup() as tg:
   224	                col_tasks = [tg.create_task(_search_collection(col)) for col in target_collections]

   162	                    set_span_attribute("results_count", 0)
   163	                    set_span_status("ok")
   164	                    return documents[:top_k]
   165	
   166	                # Map results back to documents
   167	                reranked_docs = []
   168	                for res in results:
   169	                    idx = res.get("index")
   170	                    score = res.get("relevance_score", 0.0)
   171	
   172	                    if idx is not None and 0 <= idx < len(valid_docs):
   173	                        doc = valid_docs[idx]
   174	                        doc["rerank_score"] = float(score)
   175	
   176	                        # Preserve original score if needed
   177	                        prior_score = doc.get("score")
   178	                        if "score" in doc and "vector_score" not in doc:
   179	                            doc["vector_score"] = doc["score"]
   180	
   181	                        # Update main score
   182	                        doc["score"] = float(score)
   183	                        score_provenance.stamp(
   184	                            doc,
   185	                            score_provenance.RERANKED,
   186	                            prior_score if isinstance(prior_score, (int, float)) else None,
   187	                        )
   188	                        reranked_docs.append(doc)
   189	
   190	                # Sort explicitly just in case API didn't
   191	                reranked_docs.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
   192	
   193	                set_span_attribute("results_count", len(reranked_docs))
   194	                if reranked_docs:
   195	                    set_span_attribute("top_score", round(reranked_docs[0]["score"], 4))
   196	                set_span_status("ok")
   197	
   198	                return reranked_docs[:top_k]
   199	
   200	            except Exception as e:
   201	                logger.error("❌ Re-ranking failed (Ze-Rank 2): %s", e)
   202	                set_span_status("error", str(e))
   203	                # Fallback to original order
   204	                return documents[:top_k]
   315	            doc_texts = []
   316	            valid_docs = []
   317	
   318	            for doc in documents:
   319	                text = doc.get("text") or doc.get("content") or ""
   320	                if text:
   321	                    doc_texts.append(text)
   322	                    valid_docs.append(doc)
   323	
   324	            if not doc_texts:
   325	                set_span_attribute("skipped", True)
   326	                set_span_attribute("skip_reason", "no_valid_text")
   327	                set_span_status("ok")
   328	                return documents[:top_k]
   329	
   330	            set_span_attribute("valid_documents_count", len(valid_docs))
   331	
   332	            try:
   333	                # Compute scores asynchronously
   334	                start_time = time.perf_counter()
   335	                scores = await self.compute_scores_async(query, doc_texts)
   336	                compute_time = (time.perf_counter() - start_time) * 1000
   337	                set_span_attribute("compute_time_ms", round(compute_time, 2))
   338	
   339	                # Attach scores to documents
   340	                reranked_docs = []
   341	                for doc, score in zip(valid_docs, scores, strict=False):
   342	                    # Create a copy to avoid mutating original
   343	                    doc_copy = doc.copy()
   344	
   345	                    # Store rerank score
   346	                    doc_copy["rerank_score"] = float(score)
   347	
   348	                    # Preserve original score
   349	                    prior_score = doc_copy.get("score")
   350	                    if "score" in doc_copy and "vector_score" not in doc_copy:
   351	                        doc_copy["vector_score"] = doc_copy["score"]
   352	
   353	                    # Update main score to rerank score
   354	                    doc_copy["score"] = float(score)
   355	                    score_provenance.stamp(
   356	                        doc_copy,
   357	                        score_provenance.RERANKED,
   358	                        prior_score if isinstance(prior_score, (int, float)) else None,
   359	                    )
   360	                    reranked_docs.append(doc_copy)
   361	
   362	                # Sort by rerank score (descending)
   363	                reranked_docs.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
   364	
   365	                set_span_attribute("results_count", len(reranked_docs))
   366	                if reranked_docs:
   367	                    set_span_attribute("top_score", round(reranked_docs[0]["score"], 4))
   368	                set_span_attribute("latency_ms", round(compute_time, 2))
   369	                set_span_status("ok")
   370	
   371	                logger.info(
   372	                    f"✅ Reranked {len(reranked_docs)} documents in {compute_time:.1f}ms "
   373	                    f"(model: {self.model_name})",
   374	                )

   302	def _default_kbli_dataset_path() -> Path:
   303	    """Resolve the canonical KBLI dataset in local and Fly layouts."""
   304	    module_path = Path(__file__).resolve()
   305	    for parent in module_path.parents:
   306	        for relative_path in (
   307	            Path("source_documents/KBLI_2025_FINAL_CLEAN.json"),
   308	            Path("data/source_documents/KBLI_2025_FINAL_CLEAN.json"),
   309	        ):
   310	            candidate = parent / relative_path
   311	            if candidate.is_file():
   312	                return candidate
   313	    # KBLIEye will log the missing-dataset failure and return an explicit
   314	    # DATABASE_NOT_LOADED state. Keep the expected Fly path in that log,
   315	    # without indexing beyond the shallower /app container hierarchy.
   316	    return Path("/app/source_documents/KBLI_2025_FINAL_CLEAN.json")
